```
## C. AI_007_TownEntities drowning block (53990–54629), grouped

Applicable types: town NPCs + town critters/pets. Key flags (53857–53864): `flag7` = 300 Mouse/447 GoldMouse/610 Rat; `flag8` = 616 Turtle/617 TurtleJungle/625 SeaTurtle; `flag9` = 361 Frog/445 GoldFrog/687 BoundTownSlimeYellow; `flag10` = town slimes; `flag11/12` = `flag8||flag9` (can breathe/float); `flag13/14` = town slimes.

| Lines | Effect |
|---|---|
| 53990 | `currentlyDrowning = wet && !flag11` fed into `AI_007_TownEntities_GetWalkPrediction` (56093) → forces `keepwalking = true` (never idle-stop while drowning) and scans tiles ahead: deep water/lava ahead → `avoidFalling` → turn around. |
| 54049 | `(flag9 || flag10) && wet` → frogs/town slimes leave idle: `ai[0] = 1` (walk), `ai[1] = 200+rand(500,700)`, reset ai[2]/localAI[3], netUpdate. |
| 54134, 54136 | Idle state: recompute `currentlyDrowning`, run walk prediction; then `wet && !flag11` → `Collision.DrownCollision` + `AI_007_TownEntities_CheckIfWillDrown` → switch to walking away. |
| 54293 | `flag8 && wet` → turtles' swim speed `num22 = 2f`, `num23 = 1f` (vs 0.5/0.07 dry). |
| 54306 | type 625 SeaTurtle `wet` → `num22 = 2.5f`, `num23 = 1f`. |
| 54329 | `flag13 && wet` (town slimes) → `num22 = 2f`, `num23 = 0.2f`. |
| 54334 | `flag9 && wet` (frogs) → if nearly stationary, `vx += num22*10*direction` (hop off), else `vx *= 0.9`. |
| 54406 | `wet && !flag11 && isLikeATownNPC && CheckIfWillDrown && localAI[3] <= 0` → jump-out-of-water: measure water depth above (up to 10 tiles), `vy = −sqrt(depth·g·0.3)` capped −26, set `localAI[3] = position.X` (latch), avoidFalling override. |
| 54619 | `velocity.Y < 0 && wet` → `vy *= 1.2` (rises faster while swimming up — helps clear the surface). |
| 54629 | `flag14 && !wet` (town slimes, dry) → scan tiles below for liquid/solid to decide sitting/rest behavior. |
| 55557 | `flag13 && wet` (town slimes) → bounce off walls/world-edge; `vy *= 0.9`, `vy -= 0.5`, clamp −15 (strong swim up). |
| 55574 | `flag12 && wet` (616/617/625 + 361/445/687) → `GetWaterLine` float (55588): below line → `vy -= 0.8` clamp −4 with snap; else clamp to reach line; no line → `vy -= 0.2`; wall/edge bounce; frog sets `ai[1] = 50`. |

## E. Reads outside AI (for completeness)

- 81552 `NewNPC` — **write** to a new NPC's `wet` (skip).
- 87868 `HitEffect` — 590/591 TorchZombie/ArmedTorchZombie: fire-hit dust only when `!wet`.
- 91139 `IdleSounds` — 63/64/103 jellyfish: 1-in-1000 wet-only ambient sound.
- 91936–91943 `UpdateNPC_UpdateGravity` — `wet`: shimmerWet → gravity 0.15 / maxFall 5.5; honeyWet → 0.1 / 4.0; water → 0.2 / 7.0 (universal buoyancy).
- 92939 `UpdateNPC_BuffApplyDOTs` — 59 LavaSlime with `ai[1] == 174 && lavaWet` → `lifeRegen += 32` (heals while in lava).
- 94090–94111 `FishTransformationDuringRain` (called from UpdateCollision at 93742) — 230 GoldfishWalker `wet` → Transform(55); 55 `!wet && raining` → Transform(230); 593↔592 gold variants likewise.
- 96157 `CheckDrowning` — `!lavaWet && !honeyWet && !shimmerWet` gates the drowning-bubble dust (visual only).

## Specific answers

1. **aiStyle 14 (flying)** — Yes, there IS wet handling, but it is *buoyant swim-up*, not water avoidance, and only for a hardcoded list: line 23232 (49/51/60/62/66/93/137/150/151/152/634 — the bats, Demon, Voodoo Demon, Giant Flying Fox, Spore Bat) and 23380 (48 Harpy), both doing `vy *= 0.95 (vy>0); vy -= 0.5; clamp −4; TargetClosest()`. There is also a player-wet read at 23411 (Harpy hover). Nothing else in 22895–23544 touches water — general aiStyle-14 flyers neither avoid nor float. The only flyers in the file that genuinely "escape" water are AI_114 dragonflies (`wet → velocity.Y = -3f`, line 48170).
2. **"Float on water surface"** — via `Collision.GetWaterLine` there are exactly four AI uses: 23586 & 23716 (aiStyle 16, type 688 Pufferfish only), 47435 (`GetWaterLineIterate`, AI_118 seahorses 626/627), 47885 (AI_116 water striders 612/613), 55588 (AI_007 town entities: turtles 616/617/625 and frogs 361/445/687 while wet). Additionally aiStyle 68 (363/365/603/609 swimming ducks/seagulls/grebes) floats on the surface but computes the line manually from tile liquid at 34936–34960, not with GetWaterLine. The bound NPCs 376/579 (19820) only bob upward (`vy = -0.4`) and drift — no surface-line snapping.
3. **Type 65 vs 692 in the aiStyle 16 out-of-water branch (24024)** — 65 = Shark, 692 = Orca. When beached (`velocity.Y == 0`): both get `velocity.X *= 0.94` and zero it under ±0.2 — they slide to a stop and do NOT flop. Every other aiStyle-16 fish instead does a server-side random hop (`vy = rand(−5.0..−2.0)`, `vx = rand(−2..2)*0.1`, random direction, network update). Both then take gravity `vy += 0.3` (clamp 10) and `ai[0] = 1`. Orca additionally rotates toward the player while swimming (`ai[0] == 0`, 24047) and has slower frame animation (FindFrame 75406).
4. **AI_003_Fighters and AI_001_Slimes** — Yes, both read `wet`. Fighters: 56363 (461 CreatureFromTheDeep — converts to a swimming swimmer), 56488 (586 ZombieMerman — same), 57346 (591 ArmedTorchZombie, and 590 TorchZombie in the sibling block — torch light/dust only when dry). Slimes: 61877 (all slimes — hop/buoyancy out of water; LavaSlime 59 variant clamps −10), 61479 (377/446 grasshoppers — hop only when dry), 61565/61620/61675/61755/61804 (184/535/658/659/204 — special attacks gated on `!wet`).
```
